## Simulation coverage

A card is *complete* when it has the fields the engine cannot run without, which depend on its type:

- `battlefield`: (none)
- `gear`: `energy`, `power`
- `legend`: (none)
- `rune`: (none)
- `spell`: `energy`, `power`
- `token`: (none)
- `unit`: `energy`, `power`, `might`

| OGN | 341 | 337 | 4 |
| OGS | 24 | 24 | 0 |
| SFD | 275 | 184 | 91 |
| UNL | 268 | 39 | 229 |
| **all** | **908** | **584** | **324** |

Missing field counts across incomplete cards:

- `power`: 323
- `energy`: 238
- `might`: 166
